// OpenMoHAA Vita - game text in Spanish.
//
// Each line is { "original English text" "translation" }. The game looks up the
// English text exactly as it is, spaces included, so never change the first string.
// Many entries are pieces of one sentence with a key name in between, e.g.
// "Press " + <key> + " to move forward.": keep the leading/trailing spaces and the
// " ( " / " ) " parts so the joined sentence still reads well.
//
// Encoding: UTF-8. Only Latin-1 letters can be shown (no emoji, Cyrillic, CJK).
// Double quotes cannot be used inside a string.
// Chosen in game: Vita settings (tap Select twice) > Display > Text Language.
// See docs/TRANSLATING.md to add a language.
// Machine translation: needs review by a native speaker.

// matches wins, so the more specific ones come first.

{ "Decides whether Allied Assault, Spearhead and Breakthrough share their settings or each keeps its own." "Decide si Allied Assault, Spearhead y Breakthrough comparten los ajustes o si cada uno guarda los suyos." }
{ "Off (recommended): one set for the three campaigns: the buttons, the Vita settings (graphics, HUD, sensitivity) and the Text Language. A change made in one campaign is there in the others." "No (recomendado): un conjunto para las tres campañas: los botones, los ajustes de la Vita (gráficos, HUD, sensibilidad) y el idioma del texto. Lo que cambies en una campaña vale en las demás." }
{ "On: each campaign keeps its own, as on the PC. The first time a campaign starts, it takes a copy of the current settings; after that they are separate. For example, to have other buttons in one campaign." "Sí: cada campaña guarda los suyos, como en PC. La primera vez que se inicia una campaña, copia los ajustes actuales; después quedan separados. Por ejemplo, para tener otros botones en una campaña." }
{ "It counts from the next start of the game or the next Change campaign. Nothing is deleted: when it is turned off again, the shared settings come back, and each campaign's own are kept for the next time it is on." "Vale desde el próximo inicio del juego o el próximo cambio de campaña. No se borra nada: al desactivarlo de nuevo, vuelven los ajustes compartidos, y los de cada campaña se guardan para la próxima vez que se active." }
